# Development Plan & Task Tracker

Single source of truth for development policy, milestone status, and task tracking.

## How to use
- Update status and checklist items here first.
- Add notes to the backlog rows when you complete implementation or run tests.
- Keep evidence paths and test commands in Notes where applicable.
- Codex rule: after any implementation/change, update this file in the same PR/patch.

## Update Protocol (Codex)
When a task is implemented or reviewed:
- Update the Unified Task Table status and evidence/notes.
- Update the Design Alignment Backlog row (status + notes).
- Tick the Implementation Review Checklist items that were completed.
- Add/refresh evidence paths and test commands.
- Cross-check the completed work against the active architecture doc first (`docs/architecture/fx_portfolio_operating_system.md`), then use `detailed_design_fx_signal_tool_v1.md` as a legacy reference when touching existing modules; note any deviations or confirmations in the Notes.
- Append a new entry to the Update Log with UTC time to the minute.
- Use `make update-log MSG="..."` (or `python3 tools/update_log.py "..."`) to avoid timestamp mistakes.
- If a Runbook is edited, bump its version and update last updated metadata.
- If Codex asks a clarifying question, log it in the Codex Q&A Log with default SLA (6h) and respond in Q/A format with design refs.
 - If a design ambiguity is found, log it in the Codex Q&A Log, mark Status as open, and pause the change until a response is recorded (unless explicitly instructed to proceed).

Planning rule:

- 新しい `M` を追加する時は、どの `Roadmap` を閉じる作業かを明記する
- `Roadmap` の exit criteria を変える時は、この節を先に更新する

R5 exit criteria:

- `M28-M31` がすべて `Done`
- fixed USDJPY baseline に対して candidate onboarding packet / runner / operator surface / safe promotion gate が揃っている
- onboarding result が `promote / research-only / reject` と next action 付きで artifact, GUI, ops agenda へ戻る
- unresolved suppression / rollback / runtime guardrail drift がある間は baseline 反映が自動停止する

R5 implementation slices:

- `M28 Candidate onboarding packet`
  - `candidate`, `baseline`, `standalone_result`, `marginal_contribution_result`, `shadow_readiness_result`, `eligibility_status`, `recommended_action` を 1 packet にまとめる
- `M29 Candidate onboarding runner`
  - packet を使って runbook / CLI / artifact を一気通貫で生成する
- `M30 Candidate onboarding operator surface`
  - `promote / research-only / reject`, reasons, next action を GUI / shadow / ops agenda に戻す
- `M31 Candidate onboarding safe promotion gate`
  - unresolved suppression / rollback / runtime guardrail drift がある間は baseline 反映を停止し、clear 後のみ promote を許可する

R6 exit criteria:

- `M32-M35` がすべて `Done`
- multi-pair 用の symbol metadata / exposure bucket naming / pair-scoped candidate contract が固定されている
- 最初の追加ペアで data / validation / onboarding / shadow 導線が USDJPY baseline と同じ contract で動く
- pair-cross marginal contribution と suppression / rollback 条件が operator-facing に見える

R6 implementation slices:

- `M32 Multi-pair contract`
  - symbol metadata, exposure bucket naming, pair-scoped candidate/admission contract を固定する
- `M33 First pair onboarding lane`
  - EURUSD か GBPUSD の最初の追加ペアで data / validation / candidate onboarding の導線を作る
- `M34 Cross-pair portfolio evaluation`
  - pair 横断の marginal contribution と admission comparison を回せるようにする
- `M35 Multi-pair shadow readiness`

## Release Checklist
When preparing a release, follow `docs/release_checklist.md`. This is separate from the day-to-day task tracking above.

## Implementation Review Checklist
Use this checklist after completing a task or batch.

- [x] Evidence artifacts captured (logs/reports paths noted).
- [x] Tests executed and recorded in Notes.
- [x] CLI output verified for user-facing flows.
- [x] Docs updated in this file (status + evidence).
- [x] Optional refactor/cleanup complete (if needed).
- [x] R6 multi-pair packet evidence support: focused tests added for next_symbol/data-manifest resolution and thin runner packet emission.
- [x] R6 multi-pair completion: fixed `config/portfolio_pairs.yaml` + `src/portfolio/multi_pair.py` as the single-source pair contract, generalized allocator/registry/paper-poc to pair-scoped `portfolio_group` / `exposure_bucket`, enabled `--symbols` / `--data-manifest-path` cross-pair validation, and promoted `EURUSD-first` multi-pair preparation to an operator-facing decision/gate surface in GUI/shadow/ops. Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_multi_pair.py tests/unit/test_strategy_allocation.py tests/unit/test_shadow_next_stage_runner.py tests/unit/test_run_long_horizon_portfolio_validation.py tests/unit/test_multi_pair_preparation_surface.py tests/unit/test_shadow_daily_ops.py tests/unit/test_shadow_gui_api.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py`（55 passed）, `python3 -m py_compile src/portfolio/multi_pair.py src/strategies/allocation.py src/strategies/registry.py src/backtest/paper_poc.py tools/run_long_horizon_portfolio_validation.py src/portfolio/shadow_next_stage_runner.py src/interfaces/gui/multi_pair_preparation_surface.py src/interfaces/gui/shadow_daily_ops.py src/interfaces/gui/shadow_api.py tools/gui_ops_loop.py`。
- [x] Post-qualification handoff daily evidence loop: `src/portfolio/multi_pair_post_qualification.py` を追加し、post-qualification handoff を `consistent / re_review_required / monitoring` と stable streak で ledger 化した。`src/interfaces/gui/shadow_daily_ops.py` / `src/ops/agenda.py` は `multi_pair_post_qualification_*` surface と `Re-review post-qualification handoff` task を返す。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_multi_pair_post_qualification.py tests/unit/test_shadow_daily_ops.py tests/unit/test_ops_agenda_status.py`（38 passed）, `python3 -m py_compile src/portfolio/multi_pair_post_qualification.py src/interfaces/gui/shadow_daily_ops.py src/ops/agenda.py tests/unit/test_multi_pair_post_qualification.py tests/unit/test_shadow_daily_ops.py tests/unit/test_ops_agenda_status.py`。

- [x] TwelveData latest alignment fix: TwelveData requestを `timezone=UTC` 固定にし、`timestamp` が空で `ts` のみ有効な parquet でも GUI価格CSV/signal_preview が継続動作するよう修正。`signal_preview` は最新 `*_merged.parquet` も履歴候補に追加。`pytest -q tests/unit/test_twelvedata_provider.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_signal_preview.py` と `poetry run tradectl gui loop --provider twelvedata --once --json` で確認。
- [x] GUI review hardening: `signal_preview` の複数候補読み込みで壊れたparquetをスキップ継続し、`.env` ローダーは空文字環境変数を `.env` 値で補完。`pytest -q tests/unit/test_signal_preview.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_twelvedata_provider.py tests/unit/test_update_market_data.py tests/unit/test_gui_sync.py` と `poetry run tradectl gui loop --provider twelvedata --once --json` で確認。
- [x] GUI mixed-scale data fix: `update_market_data` にシンボル帯域ベースの価格スケール正規化と `timestamp`/`ts` 合成を追加し、`*_m5_latest.parquet` を merged 更新に含めるよう修正。`_backfill_signals` は latest候補を含むデータ解決へ統一し、UIは stale 行を `historical` 表示に変更。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_update_market_data.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_sync.py tests/unit/test_signal_preview.py tests/unit/test_twelvedata_provider.py`。
- [x] GUI data freshness fix: 同期ソースディレクトリ選択を「最新バー時刻優先」に変更し、`signal_preview` は manifest 指定より新しい `*_m5_latest.parquet` を優先。`pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_sync.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_signal_preview.py` で確認。
- [x] GUI stop responsiveness fix: sync実行中の `stop` でサブプロセスを中断できるようにし、`sync_progress.state=stopped` と `stop requested` ログを返すよう改善。`pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_sync.py tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py` と `curl -sS http://127.0.0.1:8787/api/ops/status` の実地確認で検証。
- [x] GUI signal history fix: backfill対象をDonchian限定から有効化された全戦略へ拡張し、`m1_us_session_trend_pullback` の過去シグナルがGUI履歴に表示されるよう修正。`pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_sync.py` と `poetry run tradectl gui loop --provider twelvedata --once --strategy-manifest reports/gui/runtime/strategy_manifest.selected.yaml --signal-log logs/events/signal.gui.jsonl --backfill-days 2000 --json`（`backfill.appended=977`）で確認。
- [x] GUI signal candidate path fix: `source_dir` がシンボル直下のときでも `signal_preview` が `*_m5_latest.parquet` を候補に含めるよう修正し、loop-only運用での鮮度劣化を抑止。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_signal_preview.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py`。
- [x] GUI signal scope + trade-level payload fix: `/api/signals` を ops runtime の symbol/strategy 選択で絞り込み、`signal.generated` + `status=generated` のみ表示に統一。加えて `signal.generated` 出力で `entry/stop/target/level/expire_at/ttl_bars` を派生付与し、GUIカードの価格レベル欠落を解消。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_strategy_registry_contracts.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py`（31 passed）。
- [x] GUI runtime manifest weight normalization fix: 複数選択戦略で runtime manifest の enabled weight 合計が 1.0 を超えて loop 起動が失敗する問題を修正。`_materialize_runtime_manifest` に enabled weight 正規化を追加し、合計<=1.0を保証。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_strategy_registry_contracts.py`（32 passed）。
- [x] GUI strategy checklist status badges: `/api/ops/status` の `available_strategies` に `ops_state` / `ops_state_label` / `ops_state_note` を追加し、GUIで採用戦略を強調、外す戦略を灰色バッジ付きで表示。`m1_baseline_donchian_long_only` は `upper_only` と重複するため `外す` 表示へ整理。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py`（27 passed）。
- [x] GUI web polling/stop UX hardening: `ui/web/app.js` の `stop` 失敗時ボタン無効化残りを修正し、ポーリングを固定 `setInterval` から `refreshMs` 追従の `setTimeout` 再スケジュールへ変更。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py`（25 passed）。
- [x] GUI web API error handling hardening: `ui/web/app.js` の API 呼び出しを HTTP status-aware 化し、`start/stop/refresh` 失敗時に詳細理由を表示するよう修正。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_gui_web_server.py tests/unit/test_gui_ops_loop.py tests/unit/test_gui_sync.py`（30 passed）。
- [x] M3 allocator tuning evidence loop: `winner_review_summary` 由来の allocator 仮説を `src/portfolio/allocation_review.py` で共通化し、`tools/review_long_horizon_validation.py --allocation-summary-json` と `tools/evaluate_portfolio_candidates.py --allocation-summary-json` に接続。Tests: `pytest -q tests/unit/test_allocation_review.py tests/unit/test_review_long_horizon_validation.py tests/unit/test_evaluate_portfolio_candidates.py`（9 passed）, `python3 -m py_compile src/portfolio/allocation_review.py tools/review_long_horizon_validation.py tools/evaluate_portfolio_candidates.py`。

## Development Policy
Scope: new architecture and product-direction work follows `docs/architecture/fx_portfolio_operating_system.md`. `detailed_design_fx_signal_tool_v1.md` remains a legacy implementation reference for existing modules and historical design coverage.

### Portfolio OS Guardrails (2026-03-12)
- 固定方針: `strategy lab` ではなく `portfolio operating system` を作る。最適化対象は単独戦略PFではなく portfolio utility。
- 固定方針: 戦略は `alpha generator` として実装し、採否は admission layer が決める。
- 固定方針: `backtest / shadow / runtime` は同じ decision path を使う。
- 固定方針: 新戦略の採用条件は `standalone pass` に加えて `marginal contribution` を必須とする。
- 固定方針: 最初は `USDJPY-first` で進めるが、内部設計は `multi-pair-ready` に保つ。

### Personal-Use Simplification (2026-03-12)
- 多人数向けの承認フロー、sign-off テンプレ、重い監査バンドルは personal-use default では要求しない。
- 通常開発で必須なのは、設計書、`docs/development_plan.md`、実行コマンド、証跡パス、リスク停止手段である。
- broker/live の安全性に直結する kill switch, spread guard, emergency unwind, deterministic evidence は維持する。

### Superseded Policy Note
- 2026-02-11 に追記した「単体最適化優先」の guardrail は、2026-03-12 の portfolio-first 方針で superseded とする。

## Tracking Rules
- Each backlog row in the Design Alignment Backlog section gets:
  - `Status`: todo / in_progress / done
  - `Notes`: implementation refs and test commands
- The `Context` column disambiguates repeated EP IDs reused in different sections.
- Summary EP rows (e.g., `EP-01 DataLag Mitigation`) are umbrella entries and are marked `done` only after all related detailed EP rows are `done`.
- Each implementation batch updates:
  - backlog status + notes
  - relevant tests executed (record in Notes)

## Roadmap Phases (Top-Down)

### Phase 0 — Baseline & Traceability
Goal: freeze scope, identify all EP items, and set the tracking cadence.
- Backlog: Design Alignment Backlog section in this doc.
- Acceptance: backlog exists; status entries are populated for already-done items.

### Phase 1 — Core Runtime & Guardrails
Focus: EP01/EP02/EP03/EP04/EP05 items that define ingestion, determinism, guardrails, ticket UX, and reporting.
- EP01-P1..P3 (Data lag mitigation, manual CSV, resync evidence)
- EP02-P1..P3 (Determinism pipeline + replay)
- EP03-P1..P8 (Health/Spread/Kill Switch core loop + Liquidity/Dashboard items)
- EP04-P1..P3 (Ticket clarity + audit + GUI bridge)
- EP05-P1..P2 (Weekly review + benchmark)
Exit: core CLI flows and guardrails evidence are fully aligned.

### Phase 2 — Research / Stress / Journal / Benchmark
Focus: EP04/EP05/EP06 items for stress testing, trade journal, and research pipelines.
- Stress registry + engine (EP04-P1..P3)
- TradeJournal (EP04-P1..P3 in §34)
- Benchmark ingestion/replay (EP05-P1..P3)
- Research pipeline + drift (EP06-P1..P3, §26/§drift)
- Idea pipeline (EP06-IDEA-P1..P3)
- Research workspace/notebooks/artifacts (EP07-RSCH-P1..P3)
- Promotion gates (EP12-PROMO-P1..P3)
- Risk stress lab (EP12-STRESS-P1..P3)
- Experiment tracking (EP08-EXP-P1..P3)

### Phase 3 — Governance & Compliance
Focus: model risk, licensing, strategy board/lifecycle, access governance, risk consent.
- EP06-MR-P1..P4
- EP09-LIC-P1..P3
- EP09-RTF-P1..P3
- EP09-BRD-P1..P3
- EP09-LIFE-P1..P3
- EP10-COMP-P1..P3
- EP11-RISKCONSENT-P1..P3
- EP15-ACCESS-P1..P3

### Phase 4 — Ops / DocOps / Drills
Focus: Ops agenda, drills, documentation automation, readiness integrations.
- EP11-OPS-P1..P3
- EP11-DRILL-P1..P3
- EP12-DOC-P1..P5
- EP11-INC-P1..P3
- EP13-COACH-P1..P3
- EP14-DEGRADE-P1..P3

### Phase 5 — Finance / BackOffice / SecureShare
Focus: ledger/tax, evidence sharing, cost allocation.
- EP07-BO-P1..P3
- EP08-SS-P1..P3
- EP10-ACC-P1..P3
- EP05-P3..P5 (statement reconciliation)

### Phase 6 — Broker / Shadow / GUI
Focus: broker adapters, shadow flows, GUI layers.
- EP13-SHADOW-P1..P3
- EP17-BROKER-P1..P21
- EP18-GUI-P1..P3
- EP20-SHADOW-GW-P1..P3

### Phase 7 — Alpha / Experiments / Degradation / Regression
Focus: alpha loop, experimentation, degradation playbooks, regression suites.
- EP21-ALPHA-P1..P3
- EP16-REG-P1..P3

## Execution Cadence
- Implement by phase; each phase is split into batches of 3–6 EP items.
- After each batch:
  - update backlog status + notes
  - run tests listed by the design doc
  - record evidence output paths

## Start Here
1) Fill current status in the Design Alignment Backlog section.
2) Execute Phase 1 items in dependency order.
3) Continue through phases until backlog reaches `done` across all EP rows.

## M1 Implementation Status
- Source: `detailed_design_fx_signal_tool_v1.md` §0.7 M1 Core traceability table.
- Status definitions:
  - Implemented: core behavior and CLI/tests exist for M1 scope.
  - Partial: scaffolding or incomplete behavior; interfaces/tests exist.
  - Stub: placeholder with minimal logic only.
  - Not started: no matching implementation found.
- Status is based on the completion criteria listed below.
- Scope: repository code + tests only (runtime verification not performed).

## M1 Completion Criteria Checks (Design-Derived)

### FR-01/FR-02 Data ingestion & quality

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Inputs include yfinance 5m, Dukascopy HTTP burst, manual-fallback twin CSV, and `config/sla_thresholds/*.yaml`.
- [x] Normalized bars supplied to a `bar_ready_queue`/downstream buffer.
- [x] Emit `metrics/data_ingestion_sla.jsonl` with latency/429 metrics.
- [x] Emit `metrics/rate_limit_window.jsonl` with `stage_eval` entries.
- [x] Manual CSV hash audit recorded for twin CSV validation.
- [x] `health.changed` recommendations + Acceptable Degradation board-mode guidance wired to Runbook `RUN-DATA-05/06`.
- [x] Manual stage promotion/rollback requires `degraded_ack.registered` event.
- [x] Catch-up/parallelism targets (4 workers, 6 in catch-up, 30 min catch-up) enforced with Runbook gating.

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-03 Feature pipeline

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Indicator set for SMA/EMA/RSI/BB/ATR/MACD/Donchian/Zscore with per-indicator enable flags in `config/feature_pipeline.yaml`.
- [x] `FeatureFrame`/`FeatureContext` updated per symbol/timeframe with indicator cache.
- [x] `metrics/pipeline.jsonl` emitted with CPU/latency and indicator count.
- [x] ThreadPoolExecutor offload + 1h/1d resampling.
- [x] 5m bar arrival triggers delta recompute (explicit schedule).
- [x] Feature flags for M2 features exist and default false (no extra indicators enabled).
- [x] Enforce "SMA/EMA/RSI/ATR always enabled" rule beyond config defaults.
- [x] Dedicated integration test `tests/integration/test_feature_pipeline.py` (design note).

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-04 Signal engine

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Strategy registry + manifest validation with priorities/weights from `config/strategy_manifest.yaml`.
- [x] Determinism hash emitted per strategy run.
- [x] `signal.generated` events emitted.
- [x] Guarded mode suppresses new proposals and governance feature flags are honored.
- [x] Badges/score outputs reflected for downstream ticketing.

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-05 Risk manager

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Kill switch + reduce-only evaluation logic with drawdown/R_eff thresholds.
- [x] GateState integration for risk metadata and board_mode suggestion.
- [x] Thresholds sourced from `risk_policy.yaml` (0.75%/2.5%/5%) and FundingCurve input.
- [x] Spread/correlation metrics wired into risk decisions.
- [x] `risk.decision` event emission and `health.changed` notifications.
- [x] Acceptable Degradation reduce-only enforcement + Runbook gating.

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-06 Position sizing

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Fixed fractional sizing + rounding helpers exist.
- [x] PositionSizer emits size + OCO recommendations and respects broker lot/distance rules.
- [x] PositionSizer is wired into the ticket flow (draft metadata inputs for equity/risk/stop distance/ATR).

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-07 Ticket/HITL

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Ticket JSONL output + audit logging from CLI actions.
- [x] `HumanErrorChecklist` order/labels match design (`spread_window_clear` → `manual_comment_logged`).
- [x] News/Calendar/Spread gates enforced during ticket build.
- [x] Risk disclosure status surfaced in `tradectl board` header/banners.
- [x] BoardMode=guarded enforces Reduce-Only only.
- [x] TTL monitoring and missing-input warnings (OCO ack, manual comment) wired to approvals.

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-08 Mode switching

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] ModeProfile loading + ModeContext factory (`tradectl start --profile`).
- [x] Mode-specific data source/execution settings resolved from `config/profiles/*.yaml`.
- [x] HITL flow behavior is consistent across modes.
- [x] Post-resync consistency check (design calls for validation after catch-up).

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-10 Weekly report

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Weekly Markdown generated from `weekly_m1_core.md` template.
- [x] KPI single values (Sharpe/MaxDD/WinRate/CumR) extracted in CLI.
- [x] `RiskSummaryStub` is used when the M1.1 flag is disabled.
- [x] `metrics/data_ingestion_sla.jsonl` is summarized when extended blocks are enabled.
- [x] Paper 90-day warm-up sets metric_state=provisional.
- [x] Update `reports/performance/<mode>/` when `reports.performance.enable` is enabled (M1.2).

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-16/FR-18 Resync & snapshot

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Resync job queue + `catch_up_lag_minutes` metrics + `resync.completed` event emission.
- [x] Manual CSV required flag propagated in resync queue processing.
- [x] Snapshot update integrated with session/catch-up lifecycle (beyond persistence).
- [x] 20 min warning / 30 min degraded thresholds + Runbook-driven recovery enforced.
- [x] Restart-time snapshot consistency check enforced.

Assessment: Implemented.

### FR-28 Funding service

Criteria (from §0.7):
- [x] Funding CSV loader + `tradectl funding sync/status` state handling.
- [x] FundingCurve integrates Calendar triple-day adjustments and produces `swap_penalty`.
- [x] Swap/Funding values propagated into Account/Reporter outputs.
- [x] Funding evidence output to `reports/validation_log/AC-09_funding_<date>.md`.

Assessment: Implemented.

へ渡すことで、3本並行PoCは baseline `pf 1.1618 / max_dd 0.1608 / trades 2213` から `pf 1.2454 / max_dd 0.0910 / trades 642` へ改善し、`positive_year_ratio` は `0.75→1.0` になった。2026-03-13 follow-up:
